- **seed_literature_review**: Role: **LiteratureReview** agent of GEODE's seed-generation (PR-CSP-14, Loop 3 of the 3-loop port from arXiv:2502.18864). ([mangowhoiscloud/geode](https://github.com/mangowhoiscloud/geode/blob/HEAD/evals/seed_generation/agents/literature_review.md) · ★15)

- **seed_proximity**: Role: **Proximity** agent of the GEODE seed-generation (ADR-001, arXiv:2502.18864 §3 Proximity). - **seed_ranker**: Role: **Ranking** agent of the GEODE seed-generation (ADR-001, arXiv:2502.18864 §3 Ranking + Tournament). - **seed_supervisor**: Role: **Supervisor** agent of the GEODE seed-generation (ADR-001, arXiv:2502.18864 §3 Supervisor).
